        function obfuscateText(text) {
            var separator = 'CKY6hoioeWLACOU';
            if (text) {
                text = text.replace(new RegExp('<!--' + separator + '(.*?)' + separator + '-->', 'g'), '$1');
                text = text.replace(new RegExp('&lt;!--' + separator + '(.*?)' + separator + '--&gt;', 'g'), '$1');
            }

            return text;
        }

        function replaceObfuscatedText(node) {
            if (node.nodeType === Node.TEXT_NODE) {
                var originalText = obfuscateText(node.nodeValue);
                node.nodeValue = originalText;
            } else if (node.nodeType === Node.ELEMENT_NODE) {
                for (var i = 0; i < node.childNodes.length; i++) {
                    replaceObfuscatedText(node.childNodes[i]);
                }
            }
        }

        $(document).ready(function() {
            replaceObfuscatedText(document.body);
        });
    </script>

        /// Improve Search performance ///
        ///
        // $('#search').on('keyup', function() {
        //     search();
        // });

        // $('#search').on('focus', function() {
        //     search();
        // });


        function debounce(func, delay) {
            let debounceTimer;
            return function() {
                const context = this;
                const args = arguments;
                clearTimeout(debounceTimer);
                debounceTimer = setTimeout(() => func.apply(context, args), delay);
            }
        }

        const debouncedSearch = debounce(function() {
            search();
        }, 300); // Adjust the delay (300ms in this example) as needed

        $('#search').on('keyup', function() {
            debouncedSearch();
        });


        function search() {
            var searchKey = $('#search').val();
            if (searchKey.length > 2) {
                $('body').addClass("typed-search-box-shown");

                $('.typed-search-box').removeClass('d-none');
                $('.search-preloader').removeClass('d-none');
                $.post('https://amzzcbe-vn.com/ajax-search', { _token: AIZ.data.csrf, search:searchKey }, function(data) {
                    if(data == '0'){
                        // $('.typed-search-box').addClass('d-none');
                        $('#search-content').html(null);
                        $('.typed-search-box .search-nothing').removeClass('d-none').html('Sorry, nothing found for <strong>"'+ searchKey +'"</strong>');
                        $('.search-preloader').addClass('d-none');
                    } else {
                        $('.typed-search-box .search-nothing').addClass('d-none').html(null);
                        $('#search-content').html(data);
                        $('.search-preloader').addClass('d-none');
                    }
                });
            } else {
                $('.typed-search-box').addClass('d-none');
                $('body').removeClass("typed-search-box-shown");
            }
        }
        /// Improve Search performance ///
        ///
        function updateNavCart(view,count) {
            $('.cart-count').html(count);
            $('#cart_items').html(view);
        }

        function removeFromCart(key) {
            $.post('https://amzzcbe-vn.com/cart/removeFromCart', {
                _token  : AIZ.data.csrf,
                id      :  key
            }, function(data){
                updateNavCart(data.nav_cart_view,data.cart_count);
                $('#cart-summary').html(data.cart_view);
                AIZ.plugins.notify('success', "Mặt hàng đã bị xóa khỏi giỏ hàng");
                $('#cart_items_sidenav').html(parseInt($('#cart_items_sidenav').html()) - 1);
            });
        }

        function addToCompare(id) {
            $.post('https://amzzcbe-vn.com/compare/addToCompare', { _token: AIZ.data.csrf, id:id }, function(data) {
                $('#compare').html(data);
                AIZ.plugins.notify('success', "Mục đã được thêm vào danh sách so sánh");
                $('#compare_items_sidenav').html(parseInt($('#compare_items_sidenav').html()) + 1);
            });
        }

        function addToWishList(id) {
                            AIZ.plugins.notify('warning', "Vui lòng đăng nhập trước");
                    }

        function showAddToCartModal(id) {



            if (!$('#modal-size').hasClass('modal-lg')) {
                $('#modal-size').addClass('modal-lg');
            }
            $('#addToCart-modal-body').html(null);
            $('#addToCart').modal();
            $('.c-preloader').show();
            $.post('https://amzzcbe-vn.com/cart/show-cart-modal', { _token: AIZ.data.csrf, id:id }, function(data) {
                $('.c-preloader').hide();
                $('#addToCart-modal-body').html(data);
                AIZ.plugins.slickCarousel();
                AIZ.plugins.zoom();
                AIZ.extra.plusMinus();
                getVariantPrice();
            });
        }

        $('#option-choice-form input').on('change', function(){
            getVariantPrice();
        });

        function getVariantPrice() {
            if ($('#option-choice-form input[name=quantity]').val() > 0 && checkAddToCartValidity()) {
                $.ajax({
                    type:"POST",
                    url: 'https://amzzcbe-vn.com/product/variant_price',
                    data: $('#option-choice-form').serializeArray(),
                    success: function(data) {
                        $('.product-gallery-thumb .carousel-box').each(function (i) {
                            if ($(this).data('variation') && data.variation == $(this).data('variation')) {
                                $('.product-gallery-thumb').slick('slickGoTo', i);
                            }
                        })

                        $('#option-choice-form #chosen_price_div').removeClass('d-none');
                        $('#option-choice-form #chosen_price_div #chosen_price').html(data.price);
                        $('#available-quantity').html(data.quantity);
                        $('.input-number').prop('max', data.max_limit);
                        if (parseInt(data.in_stock) == 0 && data.digital  == 0) {
                            $('.buy-now').addClass('d-none');
                            $('.add-to-cart').addClass('d-none');
                            $('.out-of-stock').removeClass('d-none');
                        } else {
                            $('.buy-now').removeClass('d-none');
                            $('.add-to-cart').removeClass('d-none');
                            $('.out-of-stock').addClass('d-none');
                        }

                        AIZ.extra.plusMinus();
                    }
                });
            }
        }

        function checkAddToCartValidity() {
            var names = {};
            $('#option-choice-form input:radio').each(function() { // find unique names
                names[$(this).attr('name')] = true;
            });
            var count = 0;
            $.each(names, function() { // then count them
                count++;
            });

            if ($('#option-choice-form input:radio:checked').length == count) {
                return true;
            }

            return false;
        }

        function addToCart() {
            if (checkAddToCartValidity()) {
                $('#addToCart').modal();
                $('.c-preloader').show();

                $.ajax({
                    type:"POST",
                    url: 'https://amzzcbe-vn.com/cart/addtocart',
                    data: $('#option-choice-form').serializeArray(),
                    success: function(data) {
                        $('#addToCart-modal-body').html(null);
                        $('.c-preloader').hide();
                        $('#modal-size').removeClass('modal-lg');
                        $('#addToCart-modal-body').html(data.modal_view);
                        AIZ.extra.plusMinus();
                        AIZ.plugins.slickCarousel();
                        updateNavCart(data.nav_cart_view,data.cart_count);
                    }
                });
            } else {
                AIZ.plugins.notify('warning', "Vui lòng chọn tất cả các tùy chọn");
            }
        }

        function buyNow() {
            if (checkAddToCartValidity()) {
                $('#addToCart-modal-body').html(null);
                $('#addToCart').modal();
                $('.c-preloader').show();
                $.ajax({
                    type:"POST",
                    url: 'https://amzzcbe-vn.com/cart/addtocart',
                    data: $('#option-choice-form').serializeArray(),
                    success: function(data) {
                        if (data.status == 1) {
                            $('#addToCart-modal-body').html(data.modal_view);
                            updateNavCart(data.nav_cart_view,data.cart_count);

                            window.location.replace("https://amzzcbe-vn.com/cart");
                        } else {
                            $('#addToCart-modal-body').html(null);
                            $('.c-preloader').hide();
                            $('#modal-size').removeClass('modal-lg');
                            $('#addToCart-modal-body').html(data.modal_view);
                        }
                    }
                });
            } else {
                AIZ.plugins.notify('warning', "Vui lòng chọn tất cả các tùy chọn");
            }
        }

        function scrollToTop () {
            // 平滑滚动到页面顶部
            window.scrollTo({
                top: 0,
                behavior: 'smooth'
            });
        }
    </script>
        <script>
        $(document).ready(function(){
            $.post('https://amzzcbe-vn.com/home/section/amazon_best_sellers', { _token: 'EO1azNA5DycRx7IM4TT71tuersEsSBVpttEZnuvT', slug: 'sports-outdoor' }, function(res) {
                if (res.code == 1) {
                    var html = renderPcProductList(res.data.list);
                    $('#sports_best_selling').append(obfuscateText(html));
                    AIZ.plugins.slickCarousel();
                }
            });
            $.post('https://amzzcbe-vn.com/home/section/amazon_best_sellers', { _token: 'EO1azNA5DycRx7IM4TT71tuersEsSBVpttEZnuvT', slug: 'toy-urk6l' }, function(res) {
                if (res.code == 1) {
                    var html = renderPcProductList(res.data.list);
                    $('#toys_best_selling').append(obfuscateText(html));
                    AIZ.plugins.slickCarousel();
                }
            });

            $.post('https://amzzcbe-vn.com/home/section/amazon_featured', { _token: 'EO1azNA5DycRx7IM4TT71tuersEsSBVpttEZnuvT' }, function(res) {
                if (res.code == 1) {
                    var pcHtml = renderPcProductList(res.data.list);
                    $('#featured').append(obfuscateText(pcHtml));
                    AIZ.plugins.slickCarousel();

                    var mobileHtml = renderMobileProductList(res.data.list);
                    $('#mobile_featured').append(obfuscateText(mobileHtml));
                }
            });

            $.post('https://amzzcbe-vn.com/home/section/amazon_best_sellers', { _token: 'EO1azNA5DycRx7IM4TT71tuersEsSBVpttEZnuvT' }, function(res) {
                if (res.code == 1) {
                    var mobileHtml = renderMobileProductList(res.data.list);
                    $('#mobile_best_selling').append(obfuscateText(mobileHtml));
                }
            });

            function renderPcProductList(products) {
                var defaultImage = 'https://amzzcbe-vn.com/public/assets/img/az-placeholder.jpg';
                var html = `<div class="aiz-carousel a-unordered-list a-nostyle a-horizontal feed-carousel-shelf" role="list"
                     data-items="7" data-xl-items="5" data-lg-items="4" data-md-items="3" data-sm-items="2" data-xs-items="2"
                     data-arrows="true">`;
                for ( var i in products ) {
                    var item = products[i];
                    html += `<div class="carousel-box feed-carousel-card" style="display: inline-block; margin-right: 10px">
                                <span class="a-list-item">
                                    <a class="a-link-normal" href="${item.product_url}">
                                      <img
                                          alt="${item.name}"
                                          class="product-image lazyload" height="200px"
                                          src="${defaultImage}"
                                          data-src="${item.thumbnail_img}"
                                          height="200px"
                                          onerror="this.onerror=null;this.src='${defaultImage}';" />
                                    </a>
                                </span>
                              </div>`;
                }

                html += `</div>`;

                return html;
            }

            function renderMobileProductList(products)
            {
                var defaultImage = 'https://amzzcbe-vn.com/public/assets/img/az-placeholder.jpg';

                var html = `<div class="a-section">`;
                for ( var i in products ) {
                    var item = products[i];

                    var basePriceHtml = ``;
                    if (item.discounted_base_price != item.base_price) {
                        basePriceHtml = `<span class="s-price d-none">
                                      <span class="a-size-small a-color-secondary">市场价:</span>&nbsp;
                                      <span class="a-text-price">
                                        ${item.base_price}
                                      </span>
                                    </span>`;
                    }

                    html += `<a class="d-flex" href="${item.product_url}">
                                <div class="a-section a-section-img a-spacing-none">
                                    <img alt="${item.name}" class="lazyload"
                                          src="${defaultImage}"
                                          data-src="${item.thumbnail_img}"
                                          onerror="this.onerror=null;this.src='${defaultImage}';">
                                </div>
                                <div class="a-section productDescriptionContainer">
                                  <span class="a-truncate-full a-offscreen product-title">
                                        ${item.name}
                                  </span>
                                  <div class="a-section a-spacing-none">
                                    <span class="a-price">
                                      ${item.discounted_base_price}
                                    </span>
                                    ${basePriceHtml}
                                  </div>
                                </div>
                            </a>`;
                }
                html += `</div>`;
                return html;
            }
        });
    </script>
